# Recipe photo prompts for Copilot

1170 of 1170 recipes have no photo yet. Generated by `python3 scripts/recipe_photos.py prompts`.

Order: everyday food first, then Christmas and winter (in season now), then the rest of the year by season, shop-bought food last.

## How to do a batch

1. Open Copilot (the app or copilot.microsoft.com). Start a new chat for each batch.
2. Copy one whole line below, from "Create an image" to the end, and send it. Ask for landscape if it comes back square.
3. Look at it against the recipe. Reject it and ask again if it shows anything the recipe does not have, or meat or fish in a dish that has none.
4. Save it into a folder named after the batch, e.g. `batch-001`, as the number in front of the line: `01.png`, `02.png`... A skipped one is fine: leave its number out.
5. When a batch folder is done (or several), zip it and drop it into a Home OS chat. It is cropped to 4:3, resized, checked and committed.

The house style is inside every prompt, so each one stands on its own.
